SR-231Coordinator adjudication enters from a claimed lane and accepts only its current verdictre-accept owed

Requirement

The delivered coordinator adjudication entry point shall, regardless of the declared adjudicator retention dial, run only from a claimed lane worktree and read success only from a verdict that its own call wrote within that call's deadline. From a claimed lane, it takes only an act an accepted current verdict authorizes. The verdict, reservation, and deadline obligations stated first apply to the judging turn; the act turn's success is the completion contract stated in the acceptance criteria.

Rationale

A DERIVED requirement, and labelled so. SN-025 asks that a configured agent progress without a human curating what comes next; it does not state the entry boundary for an adjudication the coordinator starts, so this obligation arrives through two lenses. The unattended-operations lens listens for a run whose telemetry lands on trunk instead of the claimed work it judges, leaving a later reader unable to recover its work item context. The integrity lens listens for stale evidence being accepted as this call's success: a verdict left by an earlier or overdue call can make an unsuccessful current call look valid. A lane-local call that owns a fresh verdict reservation was chosen over trusting a caller-supplied path or a shared trunk record, because both leave the evidence's author and time ambiguous. Fed back to the need: SN-025's acceptance could name attributable, current adjudication evidence as part of unattended progress; until it does, this row is derived and says so. An act is authorized only by an accepted, committed verdict, so it cannot substitute later work for the judgement that authorized it.

SR-231 itself — no cell of this row changed
SR-IDSR-231
TitleCoordinator adjudication enters from a claimed lane and accepts only its current verdict
SN-RefsSN-025
Boundary-RefsB-10
Hat-RefsUNATTENDED-OPS;INTEGRITY-RECOVERABILITY
AcceptanceCriteriaAt every retention-dial value, a coordinator adjudication from the primary checkout, an unclaimed worktree or outside a repository refuses before launch; a valid claimed lane exclusively reserves a new verdict path and only one concurrent call can claim it; a successful call returns success only for a valid verdict it wrote before its deadline; and a failed, timed-out, missing or invalid verdict never returns success. From a claimed lane, a later adjudicator turn takes the act a recorded verdict authorizes from the lane head; before launch it refuses an unrecorded verdict, one owing no act, a verdict that judged a row in an unexplained state, or uncommitted lane changes, naming the relevant input. The turn reserves no verdict, writes no binding or outcome, is retained only where the verdict class is retained, and succeeds only when its call succeeds, every authorized act is taken, and verdict records remain unchanged. The reservation and verdict-success conditions stated first apply to the judging turn.

CoincidentThe need asks for unattended progress whose evidence a later reader can attribute. An adjudication that runs only from a claimed lane, and that counts as success only a verdict its own call wrote at the path it reserved before its deadline, makes the judgement the lane's and the call's, whatever else the runner wrote or left behind; and an act is taken only as an accepted, committed verdict authorizes it, so later work cannot stand in for that judgement. The row relies on nothing the runner does: it refuses what it cannot attribute. Whether the verdict judged well is the judging session's own premise, not this row's.

Phase6

SR-232Combined adjudication preserves each requested judgementre-accept owed

Requirement

Where one lane checkpoint requests several declared adjudication kinds, the delivered adjudication content shall present and validate one sitting that preserves each kind's own scope, evidence and decision grammar.

Rationale

A DERIVED requirement, and labelled so. SN-025 asks for unattended progress but does not require one sitting to carry several judgements. The unattended-operations lens catches a passing checkpoint where a requested kind was never judged; the integrity-recoverability lens catches a partial write where one kind acts while another section refuses. A composed sitting was chosen so each judgement remains independently checkable while sharing its checkpoint. Fed back to the need: SN-025's acceptance could name bounded, attributable checkpoint adjudication; until it does, this row is derived and says so.

SR-232 itself — no cell of this row changed
SR-IDSR-232
TitleCombined adjudication preserves each requested judgement
SN-RefsSN-025
Boundary-RefsB-10
Hat-RefsINTEGRITY-RECOVERABILITY;UNATTENDED-OPS
AcceptanceCriteriaFor a checkpoint requesting several declared kinds, the composed sitting identifies exactly those kinds; each kind has one scoped section that passes its own grammar, and a missing, extra, duplicate or invalid section refuses the sitting without acting on another kind's scope.
PriorityM
VerificationMethodTest
StatusAccepted
Phase6
Forminterface
CoincidentThe need asks for unattended progress that loses no judgement it asked for. A combined sitting that names exactly the kinds bound at its reservation, gives each its own scoped section in its own grammar, and is refused whole when a section is missing, extra, repeated or invalid, acting on no kind's scope, is that outcome: no checkpoint passes with a requested kind unjudged, and no kind acts beside another's refusal. Whether each section judged well is the judging session's own premise, not this row's.

Phase6

SR-234Contested or repeated review findings receive a bounded adjudicationre-accept owed

Requirement

Where a review finding is contested by the builder or coordinator, or recurs for a third review round, the delivered adjudication content shall preserve each requested finding, the position held on it and its relevant change scope, accept only a verdict that rules every requested finding exactly once as a fix, a successor row, a reasoned dismissal in a declared class, or an escalation to the operator, and direct the dispute sitting to rule out a successor row for a fail-open, data-loss, or Done-when-gap finding.

Rationale

A DERIVED requirement, and labelled so. SN-006 asks an unattended run to report failures clearly and surface to an operator when it cannot proceed, but it does not state who ends a contested or repeated review finding. The unattended-operations lens listens for an unbounded review loop in which the coordinator sends the same finding back to the builder without a bounded, attributable decision. A complete adjudication was chosen so an independent call can settle each requested finding without silently adding, dropping or defaulting one. Fed back to the need: SN-006's acceptance could state that contested review findings receive a bounded ruling; until it does, this row is derived and says so.

SR-234 itself — no cell of this row changed
SR-IDSR-234
TitleContested or repeated review findings receive a bounded adjudication
SN-RefsSN-006
Boundary-RefsB-09;B-10
Hat-RefsUNATTENDED-OPS
AcceptanceCriteriaA composed adjudication carries exactly the requested findings, each finding and held position verbatim, change-range facts, and a link to the review scope; malformed findings input refuses composition without a partial sitting. A verdict refuses when a requested finding is unruled or ruled twice, an unrequested finding is ruled, a ruling is outside the declared set, a dismissal lacks its declared reason class, or a ruling lacks its reason. A recorded dispute verdict authorizes no acceptance act. A SUCCESSOR ruling has a reason; an unknown ruling names the declared ruling set; and the shipped dispute directions rule out a successor row for a fail-open, data-loss, or Done-when-gap finding.
PriorityS
VerificationMethodTest
StatusAccepted
Phase6
Aspectprocess
Forminterface
CoincidentThe need asks that an unattended run report its failures clearly and surface to an operator when it cannot go on, rather than loop. A contested or recurring finding sent to one sitting that carries each finding and the position held on it verbatim, and whose verdict is accepted only when it rules every requested finding exactly once, from the declared rulings and with its reason, ends the dispute in a bounded ruling the lane can attribute, or in an escalation to the operator. The directions against deferring a fail-open, data-loss or Done-when-gap finding are shipped text, and recording them beside the verdict makes the standard asked of the sitting visible to the operator; whether the sitting heeds them is outside this row's check.

Phase6

SR-235Attended independent review verdicts are recorded or refusedre-accept owed

Requirement

When an attended launcher submits an independent review verdict for a work item's lane, the delivered review content shall record a verdict bound to the reviewed commit in the review-round record form the delivered verdict reporting reads, or refuse a malformed or unbound verdict without a record.

Rationale

A DERIVED requirement, and labelled so. SN-024 requires an independent critical eye and assigns the attended reviewer to the operator, but does not require that verdict to survive as review history. The integrity-and-recoverability lens listens for a durable artifact updated without an all-or-nothing guarantee: accepting an unbound or malformed verdict can associate the wrong judgement with a lane, while dropping a recorded verdict leaves the next reader with no record of the independent review. A validated record-or-refusal boundary was chosen over treating submitted text as history because the latter cannot distinguish a verdict for the reviewed commit from unrelated text. Fed back to the need: SN-024's acceptance could name a durable attended-review record; until it does, this row is derived and says so.

SR-237Blackout pauses new model work and resumes it after the windowre-accept owed

Requirement

When a declared blackout window is armed, the delivered coordination and unattended operation shall pause new model work until the window ends, preserving each claimed lane at its committed pause point and starting no model work inside the window except an adjudication needed to bring an active lane to that point, then resume eligible work after the window without retaining a session whose prior use predates that window's end.

Rationale

A DERIVED requirement, and labelled so. SN-027 requires a declared pause to stop claiming and drain work already in flight without double assignment. The unattended-operations lens identifies a high-usage interval as a pause that must not discard lane state, while the integrity lens identifies a stale retained transcript as an unsafe substitute for a fresh resume. Keeping the active claim and committed evidence was chosen over a second held state because it preserves the recovery model.

AcceptanceCriteriaAt a window boundary the end is exclusive, a wrapping interval belongs to its weekday start, and a changed declaration applies on the next reading. A new ordinary call or claim is refused during the interval; an adjudication for an active claimed work item may run to its pause point; the loop waits and retries after the interval; and the first retained keep after the interval retires a record last used before its end while a use at or after the end remains eligible to resume.

blackout_atThe blackout reading is the sole reader of the declared blackout value. It treats absent, malformed and equal endpoints as disabled, starts intervals only on weekdays, and assigns a midnight-wrapping interval to its start weekday.

Phase6

SR-239Where the reviewer retention dial is on, loop review routes retain only their scoped reviewer sessionsre-accept owed

Requirement

The delivered loop review content shall, if the reviewer setting is malformed, or names a persisted final reviewer while retention is off, read it as retention off and a fresh final reviewer; otherwise, where the declared reviewer retention dial is above zero, resume a reviewer only across iteration rounds for one lane, claim and review phase, keyed by those values and the iteration stage; read an absent, unreadable, malformed, misfiled or differently keyed retained record as no session and start fresh; never keep a reviewer session warm; retire that iteration session when the lane's full-lane round is drawn; run that round fresh, or, where the declared final-reviewer setting selects persistence, on one retained final reviewer per lane and phase that never resumes an iteration session; treat a change to the template a retained reviewer's brief is built from as a change of its governing inputs; and record session-mode and keep-scope as audit-only columns.

Rationale

A DERIVED requirement. The Performance, Unattended Operations and Integrity Recoverability lenses identify the operating cost, unattended judgement degradation, and shared-state corruption risk of repeatedly minting reviewer sessions. Repeated fresh reviewer rounds can exhaust the available plan, while scoped identity prevents one lane or review stage from inheriting another transcript. Coordinator-written review-scope and session-generation records, not reviewer-authored text, establish final-review provenance; session-mode and keep-scope remain audit-only. Fed back to the need: SN-025's acceptance could name bounded operating cost per reviewer judgement as part of what lets a run proceed with no human curating it; until it does, this row is derived and says so.

SR-239 itself — no cell of this row changed
SR-IDSR-239
TitleWhere the reviewer retention dial is on, loop review routes retain only their scoped reviewer sessions
SN-RefsSN-025
Boundary-RefsB-10
Hat-RefsPERFORMANCE;UNATTENDED-OPS;INTEGRITY-RECOVERABILITY
AcceptanceCriteriaWith the reviewer dial at zero, every review round launches fresh. With it on, two iteration rounds of one lane and phase resume one session while another lane starts its own; a record under another keying, an undeclared record shape, or a record filed under another key reads as no session; drawing the lane's full-lane round retires its iteration session; with a fresh final reviewer the full-lane round is fresh, and with a persisted one it resumes only the final reviewer's own session. A malformed reviewer setting, or a persisted final reviewer while retention is off, reads as retention off and fresh. Gate admission is specified by SR-156. Each review session log records session-mode and keep-scope as audit-only columns.

TitleCoordinator adjudication shares the keep request and refuses an unsigned account
RationaleA coordinator route that retained independently would duplicate the request composition, state path and account-home policy, so drift in either route could resume a different session or record a different governing input. Reading the credential at launch rather than retaining it makes each launch reflect the operator's current provision without exposing the value in repository state. Reading an ordinary home without creating it makes refusal leave no new state.

SR-240Commit-floor activation before agent routesre-accept owed

Requirement

Where a checkout's commit floor is inactive, the delivered agent routes shall refuse to start and name the applicable repair that activates the floor.

Rationale

A DERIVED requirement through the SECURITY lens: an agent route can create a governed commit, and an inactive commit floor leaves that action without the enforcement SN-005 expects. Start-time refusal was selected over a configurable bypass so the route cannot grant itself the authority the floor is meant to enforce.

With an active commit floor, each route starts normally. No external setting, flag or parameter disables the refusal.

Each route applies that result before it creates route-specific work, and a session launch distinguishes this refusal from a blackout refusal.

SR-241Review briefs render only over a matching verified passed-checks recordre-accept owed

Requirement

When an attended launcher requests a review brief for a lane commit, the delivered review content shall render it only when that commit, or a commit beneath it from which only record changes or no changes separate it, carries a verified record that the declared commit checks and the test set derived from the work item's requirements passed on its tree; otherwise it refuses without a brief and names the cause and the step that makes the record.

Rationale

A DERIVED requirement, and labelled so. SN-004 and SN-008 require trustworthy gate outcomes, but do not say that a metered independent-review session must be withheld when its requested tree is already known not to meet its declared commit checks. The performance lens listens for operating cost left unassessed: a review over such a tree spends the scarce review session on a failure the process can name before dispatch, while a record-only change must not require the same checks again. Trusting the verified record was chosen over running the checks during rendering because rendering then stays a bounded reader of a prior result rather than a second executor with a potentially different result. The coordinator and unattended review routes decompose this shared boundary separately. Fed back to the needs: their acceptance could state that a review begins only from a verified commit-check result.

SR-241 itself — no cell of this row changed

TitleReview briefs render only over a matching verified passed-checks record

Hat-RefsPERFORMANCE
AcceptanceCriteriaA requested review renders when its commit carries a verified passed-checks record for the test set derived from the work item's requirements, and when only record changes or no changes separate it from such a commit; it refuses without a brief when that record is absent, malformed, names another tree or parent, names another check set or test set, when a commit above it changes a non-record path, or when derivation reports no requirements, an empty set, or a partial set for code-touching work. A non-code change may render with an empty set and reported gaps. Every refusal names the reviewed commit, the cause and the step that records a passing commit checks; a test-set mismatch names both digests and a tree or parent mismatch names both values.

The record is the kit's own, read and not re-run; whether those checks are enough is their own requirements' claim.

SR-243Coordinator calls record sessions without committing outside claimed workre-accept owed

Requirement

When the coordinator's tooling launches a model session, the delivered coordinator call capability shall record the session in the usage record, record its log without committing to the shared line of development when it starts outside claimed work, refuse an unsafe resumed authoring carrier before launch, judge the call's changes against its starting state, and provide the sole launch route for author, critic and REVIEW-A calls.

Rationale

A DERIVED requirement, and labelled so. SN-026 requires attributable routing among model families, and SR-222 requires a usage record for sessions the delivered loop launches, but neither reaches a coordinator-started session or states where its record may be committed. The unattended-operations lens identifies a partial or missing call record that leaves unattended progress unrecoverable, while the integrity-and-recoverability lens identifies telemetry committed to unrelated shared work as evidence with ambiguous provenance. Recording every coordinator session through the same usage record while retaining an out-of-work log without a shared commit was chosen over leaving coordinator sessions unrecorded or treating every checkout as a commit authority. Fed back to the need: its acceptance could state that every coordinator-started model session has attributable usage and a recoverable record without changing unrelated shared work. SR-231's rationale supplies the lane-local provenance lens; its adjudication-only boundary remains unchanged.

SR-243 itself — no cell of this row changed
SR-IDSR-243
TitleCoordinator calls record sessions without committing outside claimed work
SN-RefsSN-026
Boundary-RefsB-10
Hat-RefsUNATTENDED-OPS;INTEGRITY-RECOVERABILITY
AcceptanceCriteriaFor session-log recording, claimed work includes a linked worktree whose branch tree or the shared tree carries that branch's active work, including after the local claim has closed. A coordinator-started session in such claimed work records one usage record and its log commits with that work. A session started from the shared checkout, a detached checkout, or an unclaimed branch writes its log without a shared commit. A refused record commit is reported and is never success. A resumed authoring turn continues the recorded thread and refuses before launch when its relevant record is missing, damaged, foreign, or when an uncommitted relevant record is no older than it. The edit check distinguishes an edit from another call's standard record commit, reports edits, treats unreadable history as unable to check, and never reports a refused or unable check as success. Published coordinator guidance names the coordinator-call route as the only launch path for author, critic and REVIEW-A calls, its blackout refusal and result meanings, and shared-checkout spooling.

TitleOne session service launches and records every model call
RationaleA launch or logging path per role lets a fix to what a call records reach one path and miss the others. With one path, a role differs only in data, and a new provider difference goes to its adapter rather than into each role. The caller's row is overlaid rather than trusted, so what the service measured is never shadowed by a field a role composed.

SR-244Transcript-derived usage records include coordinated nested agentsre-accept owed

Requirement

The delivered coordination capability shall record once each transcript-bearing model session that coordination completion ingests for coordinated work, including every session an agent spawns at any nesting depth, with its identity and transcript-derived usage; record no already-recorded message, duplicate no service record, grant its record no gate authority, include no transcript text, refuse an unknown source shape, and direct completion to ingest the current session and a named prior-session tail.

Rationale

A DERIVED requirement through the PERFORMANCE lens: SN-026 makes model-family routing a configurable operating choice, but does not require the cost of coordinated sessions outside the launcher result to be visible. The lens identifies an incomplete cost reading when nested work is omitted or a repeated or resumed read inflates it. Recorded probes establish that a resume appends to its existing transcript, that a Claude result excludes sub-agent usage, and that arbitrary nesting occurs; a sub-agent's final hand-back often has no stop record and is complete once the session's transcripts show the agent finished. The direct-child fan-out re-stamp is intentional: the independently observable reader, identity, contribution, recovery, duplication, shape, source-selection, record, CLI, and completion decisions must not be merged. Fed back to the need: its acceptance could require the operator to see complete, non-duplicated usage for coordinated work across the configured families.

SR-244 itself — no cell of this row changed

IF-245
the rest of this LLR — unchanged since the snapshot
LLR-IDLLR-347
SR-RefsSR-244
TitleTranscript usage is finalized once per message
DetailThe transcript reader accepts usage only from assistant message usage, orders messages by first appearance, and takes each message id's last record. A message is final when a non-null stop reason occurs, a later assistant message follows, or, for a sub-agent's last message, the session's transcripts hold a notice that the agent finished written at or after that message: a task notification naming the agent, or, for an agent its spawner waited on, the spawner's tool result for its call. A session's own transcript is never finished by a notice. Only a transcript's last message may remain open; it is named and is neither counted nor crossed by a cursor. A final message without a stop record is counted at its last record as partial, while reasoning is empty unless every counted message reports it. The usage record sums fresh input, cache read, cache write and output, selects the last non-synthetic counted model, and identifies the cli and provider.

TitleUsage ingest reports one defined command outcome per invocation
DetailThe usage-ingest command accepts an optional session identity and root. It emits per-invocation recorded, unchanged, open, skipped, refused, absent or unresolved-parent outcomes, each invocation's line naming its role, agent type and agent id, while a transcript refused for its shape is named by its file and field, in the stdout form IF-306 declares. It exits zero when no refusal, absence or record-commit refusal occurred, one when any occurred, and two when no session was selected.
